**21** And he stretched himself upon the child three times, and cried unto the Lord, and said, “O Lord my God, I pray thee, let this child’s soul come into him again.”

### 1KI 17:23 (level 1)

**23** And Elijah took the child, and brought him down out of the chamber into the house, and delivered him unto his mother: and Elijah said, “See, thy son liveth.”

### 1KI 17:24 (level 1)

**24** And the woman said to Elijah, “Now by this I know that thou art a man of God, and that the word of the Lord in thy mouth is truth.”

### 1KI 18:1 (level 1)

**1** And it came to pass after many days, that the word of the Lord came to Elijah in the third year, saying, “Go, shew thyself unto Ahab; and I will send rain upon the earth.”

### 1KI 18:5 (level 1)

**5** And Ahab said unto Obadiah, “Go into the land, unto all fountains of water, and unto all brooks: peradventure we may find grass to save the horses and mules alive, that we lose not all the beasts.”

### 1KI 18:7 (level 1)

**7** And as Obadiah was in the way, behold, Elijah met him: and he knew him, and fell on his face, and said, “Art thou that my lord Elijah?”

### 1KI 18:15 (level 1)

**15** And Elijah said, “As the Lord of hosts liveth, before whom I stand, I will surely shew myself unto him to day.”

### 1KI 18:17 (level 1)

**17** And it came to pass, when Ahab saw Elijah, that Ahab said unto him, “Art thou he that troubleth Israel?”

### 1KI 18:21 (level 1)

**21** And Elijah came unto all the people, and said, “How long halt ye between two opinions? if the Lord be God, follow him: but if Baal, then follow him.” And the people answered him not a word.

### 1KI 18:24 (level 1)

**24** And call ye on the name of your gods, and I will call on the name of the Lord: and the God that answereth by fire, let him be God.” And all the people answered and said, “It is well spoken.”

### 1KI 18:25 (level 1)

**25** And Elijah said unto the prophets of Baal, “Choose you one bullock for yourselves, and dress it first; for ye are many; and call on the name of your gods, but put no fire under.”

### 1KI 18:26 (level 1)

**26** And they took the bullock which was given them, and they dressed it, and called on the name of Baal from morning even until noon, saying, “O Baal, hear us.” But there was no voice, nor any that answered. And they leaped upon the altar which was made.

### 1KI 18:27 (level 1)

**27** And it came to pass at noon, that Elijah mocked them, and said, “Cry aloud: for he is a god; either he is talking, or he is pursuing, or he is in a journey, or peradventure he sleepeth, and must be awaked.”

### 1KI 18:30 (level 1)

**30** And Elijah said unto all the people, “Come near unto me.” And all the people came near unto him. And he repaired the altar of the Lord that was broken down.

### 1KI 18:31 (level 1)

**31** And Elijah took twelve stones, according to the number of the tribes of the sons of Jacob, unto whom the word of the Lord came, saying, “Israel shall be thy name:”

### 1KI 18:34 (level 1)

**34** And he said, “Do it the second time.” And they did it the second time. And he said, “Do it the third time.” And they did it the third time.

### 1KI 18:39 (level 1)

**39** And when all the people saw it, they fell on their faces: and they said, “The Lord, he is the God; the Lord, he is the God.”

### 1KI 18:40 (level 1)

**40** And Elijah said unto them, “Take the prophets of Baal; let not one of them escape.” And they took them: and Elijah brought them down to the brook Kishon, and slew them there.

### 1KI 18:41 (level 1)

**41** And Elijah said unto Ahab, “Get thee up, eat and drink; for there is a sound of abundance of rain.”

### 1KI 18:43 (level 1)

**43** And said to his servant, “Go up now, look toward the sea.” And he went up, and looked, and said, “There is nothing.” And he said, “Go” again seven times.

### 1KI 18:44 (level 1)

**44** And it came to pass at the seventh time, that he said, “Behold, there ariseth a little cloud out of the sea, like a man’s hand.” And he said, “Go up, say unto Ahab, ‘Prepare thy chariot, and get thee down, that the rain stop thee not.’”

### 1KI 19:2 (level 1)

**2** Then Jezebel sent a messenger unto Elijah, saying, “So let the gods do to me, and more also, if I make not thy life as the life of one of them by to morrow about this time.”

### 1KI 19:4 (level 1)

**4** But he himself went a day’s journey into the wilderness, and came and sat down under a juniper tree: and he requested for himself that he might die; and said, “It is enough; now, O Lord, take away my life; for I am not better than my fathers.”

### 1KI 19:5 (level 1)

**5** And as he lay and slept under a juniper tree, behold, then an angel touched him, and said unto him, “Arise and eat.”

### 1KI 19:7 (level 1)

**7** And the angel of the Lord came again the second time, and touched him, and said, “Arise and eat; because the journey is too great for thee.”

### 1KI 19:10 (level 1)

**10** And he said, “I have been very jealous for the Lord God of hosts: for the children of Israel have forsaken thy covenant, thrown down thine altars, and slain thy prophets with the sword; and I, even I only, am left; and they seek my life, to take it away.”

### 1KI 19:13 (level 1)

**13** And it was so, when Elijah heard it, that he wrapped his face in his mantle, and went out, and stood in the entering in of the cave. And, behold, there came a voice unto him, and said, “What doest thou here, Elijah?”

### 1KI 19:14 (level 1)

**14** And he said, “I have been very jealous for the Lord God of hosts: because the children of Israel have forsaken thy covenant, thrown down thine altars, and slain thy prophets with the sword; and I, even I only, am left; and they seek my life, to take it away.”

### 1KI 19:20 (level 1)

**20** And he left the oxen, and ran after Elijah, and said, “Let me, I pray thee, kiss my father and my mother, and then I will follow thee.” And he said unto him, “Go back again: for what have I done to thee?”

### 1KI 19:20 (level 1)

**20** And he left the oxen, and ran after Elijah, and said, “Let me, I pray thee, kiss my father and my mother, and then I will follow thee.” And he said unto him, “Go back again: for what have I done to thee?”

### 1KI 20:7 (level 1)

**7** Then the king of Israel called all the elders of the land, and said, “Mark, I pray you, and see how this man seeketh mischief: for he sent unto me for my wives, and for my children, and for my silver, and for my gold; and I denied him not.”

### 1KI 20:8 (level 1)

**8** And all the elders and all the people said unto him, “Hearken not unto him, nor consent.”

### 1KI 20:10 (level 1)

### 1KI 20:14 (level 1)

**14** And Ahab said, “By whom?” And he said, “Thus saith the Lord,” “‘Even by the young men of the princes of the provinces.’” Then he said, “Who shall order the battle?” And he answered, “Thou.”

### 1KI 20:17 (level 1)

**17** And the young men of the princes of the provinces went out first; and Ben–hadad sent out, and they told him, saying, “There are men come out of Samaria.”

### 1KI 20:18 (level 1)

### 1KI 20:22 (level 1)

**22** And the prophet came to the king of Israel, and said unto him, “Go, strengthen thyself, and mark, and see what thou doest: for at the return of the year the king of Syria will come up against thee.”

### 1KI 20:31 (level 1)

**31** And his servants said unto him, “Behold now, we have heard that the kings of the house of Israel are merciful kings: let us, I pray thee, put sackcloth on our loins, and ropes upon our heads, and go out to the king of Israel: peradventure he will save thy life.”

### 1KI 20:32 (level 1)

**32** So they girded sackcloth on their loins, and put ropes on their heads, and came to the king of Israel, and said, “Thy servant Ben–hadad saith, ‘I pray thee, let me live.’” And he said, “Is he yet alive? he is my brother.”

### 1KI 20:33 (level 1)

**33** Now the men did diligently observe whether any thing would come from him, and did hastily catch it: and they said, “Thy brother Ben–hadad.” Then he said, “Go ye, bring him.” Then Ben–hadad came forth to him; and he caused him to come up into the chariot.

### 1KI 21:3 (level 1)

**3** And Naboth said to Ahab, “The Lord forbid it me, that I should give the inheritance of my fathers unto thee.”

### 1KI 21:4 (level 1)

### 1KI 21:5 (level 1)

### 1KI 22:15 (level 1)

If it be, give me thine hand.” And he gave him his hand; and he took him up to him into the chariot.

### 2KI 10:24 (level 1)

### 2KI 13:18 (level 1)

### JAS 4:15 (level 1)

### 1PE 1:16 (level 1)
